A no-key test proves plumbing; only a with-key run proves the agent works against a real model. Cover file-writing prompts, multi-turn conversations, tool use, and mid-stream cancellation. Highest-value are **smoke tests** that boot the real example, send one prompt, and check the world — they catch the \"green unit tests, broken product\" class that mocks cannot ([postmortem 0001](postmortem/0001-acp-default-export-drops-inject.md)). Self-skip keeps secretless CI and keyless contributors unblocked; it is not a cost signal. Every example ships keyless and with-key smokes ([examples/AGENTS.md](../examples/AGENTS.md)).\n20\t\n21\t## Prefer the real implementation over a mock\n22\t\n23\tMock only the expensive or non-deterministic boundary (LLM adapter, network, clock); keep everything downstream real. A hand-rolled stand-in proves the bridge moves bytes, not that the shipping tool behaves as asserted. Bridge tool-call tests use the scripted mock model with the real tool and executor: `makeBridgeHarness({ withBash: true })` plugs in `dsh-bash-local` and `dsh-tool-bash`, then runs `echo`.\n24\t\n25\tRecovery tests separate pre/post-chunk failures by step and prove failed chunks derive no message or tool side effect. Cover exhaustion, cancellation, policy composition, persistence, status, wire counts, transport-closing idle timeouts, and shipping Loader composition.\n26\t\n27\t## Verify the world, not the self-report\n28\t\n29\tAn e2e assertion re-runs the command or re-reads the file externally; a keyword probe on the agent's own output lets a cheating agent pass. Assert untouched files are byte-identical. e2e tests own their resources: create the harness in the test, dispose in `afterEach` (even on failure/retry/timeout); shared fixtures live in a plain `tests/harness.ts`, never another `*.e2e.ts` (importing a spec re-registers its `describe` and duplicates real API calls).\n30\t\n31\t## Test the real entry path\n32\t\n33\t- Product-visible plugins require a non-unit REAL-composition test. Hand-built `ctx.plugin(...)` suites are insufficient: boot test-only `cordis.yml` through Loader and app/process, mock only external services or nondeterministic inputs, and assert model-visible request/log, durable state, or user-visible output. Keep opt-ins out of shipped defaults.\n34\t- A guard only guards if the regression actually fails it. For a plugin without `inject` (bundle/composition plugins), a Loader smoke stays green when a default export replaces the required named exports — add an explicit `expect('default' in mod).toBe(false)` plus an `unwrapExports` round-trip assertion, and prove it: introduce the regression, watch red, revert.\n35\t- \"Real entry path\" means the published artifact: a package `bin` runs built `lib/bin.js` under plain `node`, exposing failures tsx masks (settle races, module resolution, swallowed load failures). The same applies to non-index runtime entries (the worker-thread sibling `lib/worker.cjs`) and singleton modules shared across bundles (`packages/sdk/server/tests/built-scope-carrier.e2e.ts`). Keep the built-artifact smokes green (`packages/examples/*/tests/built-bin.e2e.ts`, `packages/code-runtime/code-runtime-worker-thread/tests/built-lib.e2e.ts`), and assert a genuinely-missing config exits non-zero.\n36\t\n37\t## Test resolution: source plane only\n38\t\n39\t- Every vitest config points vite-tsconfig-paths at `tsconfig.base.json`; bare workspace imports resolve to `src` ([layout](development.md#typescript-project-layout)), never through package `exports` to built `lib/` — stale artifacts there load a second copy of module singletons. Built artifacts are consumed only explicitly: `lib`-mode subprocesses and the built smokes below.\n40\t\n41\t## Test subprocess launch modes\n42\t\n43\t- CI and build-having test lanes run every example or Cordis-config subprocess from built `lib/` through the shared dual-mode launcher. Do not hand-write `--import tsx` for these subprocesses.\n44\t- Protocol and operating-system fixtures that do not load Cordis run erasable `.ts` directly with Node, without tsx or the root paths map.\n45\t- Only a test whose subject is source-path resolution may select `src`; state that contract in the test.\n46\t\n47\t## When a snapshot test is required\n48\t\n49\tEvery non-trivial model-, protocol-, or human-visible change adds or updates a keyless scenario in the same PR through a runnable example's owning snapshot suite. Package tests, e2e assertions, mock/test-only compositions, and PR rationale do not replace the assembled transcript; extend the harness when needed. ACP automation scenarios use `examples/<name>/tests/snapshots/`, a scenario table over the [`dsh-acp-snapshot`](../packages/test-support/acp-snapshot/README.md) suite factory (`examples/acp-agent` is primary); `examples/headless-agent` owns the internal canonical-event JSONL snapshots and replay fixtures. Browser-rendered web GUI journeys use `apps/web/tests/snapshots/`. bundles\n  workflow/    workflow capability + worker-thread provider + tool Consumer\n  todo/        todo_write tool\n  plan/        plan mode as logged state\n  preset/      per-session agent composition from preset cordis.yml files\n  guard/       loop-hygiene, tool-timeout, session-budget plugins\n  self-modification/  the agent inspects/mounts its own plugins\n  hooks/       Claude Code/Codex hook bridges + wire-protocol library\n  session/     durable session data: persistence, projection, titles, telemetry\n  identity/    anonymous identity\n  settings/    user-settings capability + file provider\n  credentials/ credential-reference capability + env/.env provider\n  acp/         automation-only Agent Client Protocol server\n  interaction/ approval/interaction capabilities, permission, commands, ask-user\n  boot/        shared app-bin glue\n  sdk/         JSON-RPC protocol, server, and TypeScript client\n  examples/    demo bundles (agent-spine + CLI/ACP/JSON-RPC bins)\n  support/     dev/test infrastructure\n  util/        zero-dependency utilities\npython/      Python SDK and bundled runtime (see python/README.md)\nnative/      @deepseek-ai/node-addon-landlock-run source of record (see native/README.md)\nexamples/    Runnable cordis.yml leaves over packages/examples bundles (see examples/AGENTS.md)\n.agents/     Agent workflows and Agent Notes (`notes/`)\ndocs/        architecture, generated catalogs, postmortems, cookbook (see docs/AGENTS.md)\nscripts/     repo gates and generators\nwebsite/     VitePress projection of selected bilingual docs/ sources\ndata/        this repository's own transcripts, runs, knowledge packs (see data/README.md)\n```\n\nPackage groups: [packages/README.md](packages/README.md).\n\n## Commands\n\n```sh\npnpm install            # pnpm workspaces, node ^22.19 || >=24\npnpm run clean           # remove build outputs and residue of deleted packages\npnpm run test           # vitest unit tests\npnpm run test:coverage  # CI coverage gate: per-file 100% on packages/*/*/src\npnpm run test:e2e       # real-API tests; self-skip without DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\npnpm run test:snapshot  # keyless ACP/headless replay vs expected outputs; filter: -t <name>\npnpm run test:snapshot:record  # re-record expected outputs (needs key)\npnpm run typecheck\npnpm run lint\npnpm run duplication    # cross-file TypeScript clone detection\npnpm run build          # tsc emits lib/types, tsdown bundles runtime\npnpm run hygiene        # knip + publint + workspace constraints + NodeNext consumer check\npnpm run check:windows-wine  # ONLY when diagnosing a known Windows failure (needs wine); CI owns it\npnpm run doc-sync       # all documentation gates; leaf list in scripts/run-gates.ts\npnpm run website:build  # VitePress build (doubles as dead-link check)\npnpm dsh --profile headless \"task\"  # run one task from source (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\npnpm run demo:cordis    # the agent modifies its own runtime (needs key)\npnpm run demo:acp       # ACP automation server (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\n```\n\n### Host sandbox failures\n\nWhen `gh`, `pnpm`, build, test, or generator commands fail because the agent sandbox blocks credentials, network, IPC, file watching, or nested `sandbox-exec`, retry unchanged with the narrowest host escalation before diagnosing authentication or project failure. Require sandbox evidence; never bypass genuine test failures or the product sandbox under test.\n\n### Run relevant checks locally\n\nRun checks before pushes via [dsh-pre-push-checks](.agents/skills/dsh-pre-push-checks/SKILL.md); report only commands run. After `gh stack sync`, validate immediately; merge only after checks pass.\n\n- Match evidence to the surface: focused tests for behavior, snapshots for model or user output, `doc-sync` for docs, build/hygiene and built smokes for published paths, real-API e2e for providers.\n- Never default to the full suite or repeat a passing check for commit or push. CI owns exhaustive coverage and the platform matrix; rehearse all locally only by explicit request, for CI diagnosis, or an irreducibly repository-wide change.\n- `test:coverage`, not `test`, is the CI coverage gate ([why](docs/testing.md)).\n\n## Secrets / .env\n\nReal-API tests and demos read `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, optional `DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL`, and root `.env`. cordis.yml allows `!!js` (never `!js`) under plugin `config` and entry `disabled`; other metadata stays literal, so conditional composition also uses overlays ([primer](docs/cordis-primer.md#loader-configuration)). Never commit credentials. CI e2e skips without a key; [testing.md](docs/testing.md) owns key policy.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- Every npm package is `@deepseek-ai/dsh-<name>`; vendored packages are rescoped ([mapping](docs/rescope.md)) and `private: true`. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` is a peerDependency (+ dev) of every harness package.\n- ESM everywhere (`\"type\": \"module\"`). Use package names across packages and `.ts` in local relative imports. Config subprocesses run built `lib/` under plain Node; source regressions use their declared launcher ([testing policy](docs/testing.md#test-subprocess-launch-modes)). The `dsh` CLI source launch runs through tsx's ESM-only hook (`node --import tsx/esm`); modules it reaches must stay ESM (no CJS-only exports) — Node's native TypeScript modes are unavailable across the engines range ([source-launch contract](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-07-29-dsh-source-launch-tsx-esm.md)). Raw/Web `cordis.yml` bare plugins must appear in their resolver manifest's `dependencies`; `verify-cordis-config` enforces it.\n- **Registrations are effects**: every contribution goes through `ctx.effect()` / `ctx.on()`; a registry's `register()` returns the disposer.\n- **Runtime invariants assert owned relationships.** Check authoritative event streams or mutable data, not service or method presence, plugin metadata or effects, or fixed pure examples. Without a plausible relationship, an explained empty companion is correct ([package invariant rules](packages/AGENTS.md)).\n- **Typed events use declaration merging** and merge-extensible maps. Event JSDoc needs `@mode` and payload `@param`; scoped keys absent from payloads need `@dshScopeScan unsupported`. Public service methods document parameters and non-void returns. A `SessionEventMap` member is required-on-read by default — builds that do not know its type refuse the log unless the event carries the envelope's `ignorable: true`; only structural format changes bump `SESSION_FORMAT_VERSION` ([mechanism](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-08-10-session-log-version-mechanism.md)).\n- **Switch on discriminant tags.** Closed unions end in `assertNever`; merge-extensible unions fall through a documented default.\n- **Waterfall listeners MUST call `next()`** to delegate; returning without it short-circuits the chain ([semantics](docs/cordis-primer.md#cordis-waterfall-semantics)).\n- **Model-visible ⟺ logged**: anything that reaches a model request must be reconstructable from the session log; a new model-visible input requires a session event.\n- **Plugins, not loop changes**: new behavior goes on documented extension points; changing `agent-loop` requires updating docs/architecture.md.\n- **A capability seam comprises Service Definition / Service Provider / Consumer roles.** It is complete, never one role; split only when roles evolve independently ([glossary](docs/glossary.md#capability-seam)).\n- **Prefer maintained dependencies over hand-rolling** when they genuinely delete owned code and tests ([policy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-07-26-dependencies-over-hand-rolling.md)).\n- **Explicit > implicit at package boundaries**: defaulting is an explicit `resolve(request): Spec` step in the owning implementation, never a hidden `?? default` inside `run()` (the `dsh-shell` request/spec split is the template).\n- **No hardcoded tunables in plugins**: deployment-varying choices are validated `Config` fields changeable from cordis.yml; a `DEFAULT_*` constant or test hook is not configurability. Protocol constants, external specs, and security invariants stay fixed.\n- **Misconfiguration fails loud** at load when self-contained, otherwise at the earliest resolvable point; never silently skip a missing referent.\n- **Opaque cross-boundary ids are branded** (`Branded<B>` from `dsh-brand`), never bare `string`.\n- **Trust TypeScript at typed same-process boundaries.** Do not add runtime validation, fallback behavior, or hostile-input tests solely for values the static interface requires; validate at parser/config, queued, model/tool JSON, durable/file, worker, process, and wire boundaries.\n- **Source plane vs artifact plane, never mixed.** Static gates and tests resolve workspace imports through tsconfig `paths` to `src` and pass on a clean tree; gates consuming built `lib/` declare that dependency ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **Keep compiler faces explicit.** Each package uses one aggregate except `api/remotes`; repo-wide programs seed a face config, never the root solution ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **An empty `catch` names what it swallows** and why nothing else can reach it; keep the `try` to one statement.\n- Do not comment on facts obvious from code.\n- **Prefer symmetry for parallel values**; unexplained asymmetry usually signals a missed extraction.\n- **Tests describe behavior, not correctness.** Change obsolete behavior with its tests; explain why in the PR.\n- **Non-trivial changes MUST include an Agent Note in the same PR;** only mechanical/local edits are exempt ([scope](.agents/notes/README.md#when-to-write-one)). Archived notes are frozen: never edit or treat them as current authority ([archive policy](.agents/notes/README.md#archiving-and-deletion)).\n- **Testing policy** — [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md). Every non-trivial model- or product-user-visible behavior change adds or updates a keyless snapshot through a real runnable example in the same PR; package tests, e2e-only assertions, and mock-only fixtures do not substitute for the assembled application transcript. Fixtures must replay on macOS/Linux; fix fixtures, not normalizers.\n- **A tool's UI render intent is part of its design**, decided up front (`generic`/`terminal`/`diff`, `locations`); presentation methods are pure functions of `args` ([cookbook](docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md)).\n- **Plan unit, e2e, and snapshot coverage** for capability seams, lifecycle paths, and transcript output; include missing snapshot-harness support in the same change.\n- **Choose PR history deliberately.** Split independent changes; fix the introducing PR before propagation. Standalone PRs and official stacks may merge-forward or rebase after review. Rewrites use `--force-with-lease`, abort on remote movement, never raw `--force`; an in-progress merge-forward preserves its checkpoint before taking a newer base ([rationale](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-02-native-github-stacks-and-optional-rebases.md)).\n- **Labels:** one PR `kind/*`, all material `area/*`, and native Issue Type ([taxonomy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-08-unified-github-label-taxonomy.md)).\n- TODO markers: `FIXME`/`TODO`/`XXX` by urgency ([semantics](docs/development.md)).\n- Files end with exactly one trailing newline; `git diff --cached --check` (pre-commit) gates it.\n\n## Defensive patterns\n\nRead [docs/defensive-patterns.md](docs/defensive-patterns.md) before lifecycle, concurrency, subprocess, or teardown work.\n\n## Type safety and documentation\n\nEverything compiles under `strict: true` with `noImplicitAny`; every remaining `any` explains why narrowing is infeasible. Every module and export has concise JSDoc for its non-obvious contract; function-like exports include `@param`/`@returns`, as enforced by `verify-export-jsdoc`. Heritage-declared members, plugin-protocol slots, and constructors keep their docs at the declaring Service Definition, protocol, or class.\n\nComments and docs state complete contracts and context, not reasoning transcripts. Use direct, concrete terms. Do not use metaphors. Before writing `contract`, `boundary`, or `shape`, ask whether a more exact term names the subject: write `response fields`, `JSON validation`, or `ESM exports` instead of `response shape`, `validation boundary`, or `module shape`. Keep `contract` for preconditions, postconditions, invariants, compatibility promises, and other obligations that callers, callees, implementers, providers, producers, or consumers rely on. Keep a literal process, wire, security, transaction, or lifecycle boundary. Do not narrate control flow or tests, preserve review history, or restate code. Keep behavior, failure, timing, ownership, and safe-use facts; link the rationale. Use [dsh-prose-standard](.agents/skills/dsh-prose-standard/SKILL.md) for decisions. Wire mechanically checkable invariants into an executed top-level gate and prove each changed acceptance path rejects an invalid case. Use narrow, justified exceptions instead of disabling a rule globally.\n\nDocs accompany every code change: update affected README and JSDoc contracts together. Routine bilingual work follows [docs/AGENTS.md](docs/AGENTS.md); only explicit user invocation may run `dsh-translate-docs`. Current-state prose, one physical line per paragraph, one home per fact, and word budgets live there.\n\n## Editing these instructions\n\n`CLAUDE.md` symlinks `AGENTS.md` at root, `packages/`, and `examples/`; edit the real file. Keep each rule self-contained while linking high-level docs. Condense when clarity survives; raise a `verify-doc-budgets` ceiling when required content genuinely needs the space.\n\n## Vendoring policy\n\n`vendor/` packages are pinned source copies (manifest with upstream SHAs in [vendor/README.md](vendor/README.md)). Update via its sync procedure; re-apply or retire logged local modifications; rerun `pnpm run test && pnpm run build`."}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"06071a52-fbbd-4cb1-9558-bfc6726b0b09","timestamp":"2026-09-08T01:23:11.734Z","rendered":[{"content":"<system-reminder>\nCodebase and user instructions are shown below. ^22.19 || >=24\n63\tpnpm run clean           # remove build outputs and safe residue from deleted packages\n64\tpnpm run test           # vitest unit tests\n65\tpnpm run test:coverage  # CI coverage gate: per-file 100% on packages/*/*/src\n66\tpnpm run test:e2e       # real-API tests; self-skip without DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\n67\tpnpm run test:snapshot  # keyless ACP/headless replay vs expected outputs; filter: -t <name>\n68\tpnpm run test:snapshot:record  # re-record expected outputs (needs key)\n69\tpnpm run typecheck\n70\tpnpm run lint\n71\tpnpm run duplication    # cross-file TypeScript clone detection\n72\tpnpm run build          # tsc emits lib/types, tsdown bundles runtime\n73\tpnpm run hygiene        # knip + publint + workspace constraints + NodeNext consumer check\n74\tpnpm run check:windows-wine  # ONLY when diagnosing a known Windows failure (needs wine); CI owns this signal\n75\tpnpm run doc-sync       # all documentation gates; leaf list in scripts/run-gates.ts\n76\tpnpm run website:build  # VitePress build (doubles as dead-link check)\n77\tpnpm dsh --profile headless \"task\"  # run one task from source (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\n78\tpnpm run demo:cordis    # the agent modifies its own runtime (needs key)\n79\tpnpm run demo:acp       # ACP automation server (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\n80\t```\n81\t\n82\t### Host sandbox failures\n83\t\n84\tWhen required `gh`, `pnpm`, build, test, or generator commands fail because the agent sandbox blocks credentials, network, IPC, file watching, or nested `sandbox-exec`, retry unchanged with the narrowest host escalation before diagnosing authentication or project failure. Require sandbox evidence; never bypass genuine test failures or the product sandbox under test.\n85\t\n86\t### Run relevant checks locally\n87\t\n88\tRun checks before pushes via [dsh-pre-push-checks](.agents/skills/dsh-pre-push-checks/SKILL.md); report only commands run. After `gh stack sync`, validate immediately; do not merge before checks pass.\n89\t\n90\t- Match evidence to the surface: focused tests for behavior, snapshots for model or user output, `doc-sync` for docs, build/hygiene and built smokes for published paths, and real-API e2e for provider behavior.\n91\t- Never default to the full suite or repeat a passing check for commit or push. CI owns exhaustive coverage and the platform matrix; rehearse all locally only by explicit request, for CI diagnosis, or for an irreducibly repository-wide change.\n92\t- `test:coverage`, not `test`, is the CI coverage gate ([why](docs/testing.md)).\n93\t\n94\t## Secrets / .env\n95\t\n96\tReal-API tests and demos read `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, optional `DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL`, and root `.env`. cordis.yml allows `!!js` (never `!js`) under plugin `config` and entry `disabled`; other metadata stays literal, so conditional composition also uses overlays ([primer](docs/cordis-primer.md#loader-configuration)). Never commit credentials. CI e2e skips without a key; [testing.md](docs/testing.md) owns key policy.\n97\t\n98\t## Conventions\n99\t\n100\t- Every npm package is `@deepseek-ai/dsh-<name>`; vendored packages are rescoped ([mapping](docs/rescope.md)) and `private: true`. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` is a peerDependency (+ dev) of every harness package.\n101\t- ESM everywhere (`\"type\": \"module\"`). Use package names across packages and `.ts` in local relative imports. Config subprocesses run built `lib/` under plain Node; source regressions use their declared launcher ([testing policy](docs/testing.md#test-subprocess-launch-modes)). The `dsh` CLI source launch runs through tsx's ESM-only hook (`node --import tsx/esm`); modules it reaches must stay ESM (no CJS-only exports) — Node's native TypeScript modes are unavailable across the engines range ([source-launch contract](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-07-29-dsh-source-launch-tsx-esm.md)). Raw/Web `cordis.yml` bare plugins must appear in their resolver manifest's `dependencies`; `verify-cordis-config` enforces it.\n102\t- **Registrations are effects**: every contribution goes through `ctx.effect()` / `ctx.on()`; a registry's `register()` returns the disposer.\n103\t- **Runtime invariants assert owned relationships.** Check authoritative event streams or mutable data, not service or method presence, plugin metadata or effects, or fixed pure examples. Without a plausible relationship, an explained empty companion is correct ([package invariant rules](packages/AGENTS.md)).\n104\t- **Typed events use declaration merging** and merge-extensible maps. Event JSDoc needs `@mode` and payload `@param`; scoped keys absent from payloads need `@dshScopeScan unsupported`. Public service methods document parameters and non-void returns. A `SessionEventMap` member is required-on-read by default — builds that do not know its type refuse the log unless the event carries the envelope's `ignorable: true`; only structural format changes bump `SESSION_FORMAT_VERSION` ([mechanism](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-08-10-session-log-version-mechanism.md)).\n105\t- **Switch on discriminant tags.** Closed unions end in `assertNever`; merge-extensible unions fall through a documented default.\n106\t- **Waterfall listeners MUST call `next()`** to delegate; returning without it short-circuits the chain ([semantics](docs/cordis-primer.md#cordis-waterfall-semantics)).\n107\t- **Model-visible ⟺ logged**: anything that reaches a model request must be reconstructable from the session log; a new model-visible input requires a session event.\n108\t- **Plugins, not loop changes**: new behavior goes on documented extension points; changing `agent-loop` requires updating docs/architecture.md.\n109\t- **A capability seam comprises Service Definition / Service Provider / Consumer roles.** It is complete, never one role; split only when roles evolve independently ([glossary](docs/glossary.md#capability-seam)).\n110\t- **Prefer maintained dependencies over hand-rolling** when they genuinely delete owned code and tests ([policy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-07-26-dependencies-over-hand-rolling.md)).\n111\t- **Explicit > implicit at package boundaries**: defaulting is an explicit `resolve(request): Spec` step in the owning implementation, never a hidden `?? default` inside `run()` (the `dsh-shell` request/spec split is the template).\n112\t- **No hardcoded tunables in plugins**: deployment-varying choices are validated `Config` fields changeable from cordis.yml; a `DEFAULT_*` constant or test hook is not configurability. Protocol constants, external specs, and security invariants stay fixed.\n113\t- **Misconfiguration fails loud** at load when self-contained, otherwise at the earliest resolvable point; never silently skip a missing referent.\n114\t- **Opaque cross-boundary ids are branded** (`Branded<B>` from `dsh-brand`), never bare `string`.\n115\t- **Trust TypeScript at typed same-process boundaries.** Do not add runtime validation, fallback behavior, or hostile-input tests solely for values the static interface requires; validate at parser/config, queued, model/tool JSON, durable/file, worker, process, and wire boundaries.\n116\t- **Source plane vs artifact plane, never mixed.** Static gates and tests resolve workspace imports through tsconfig `paths` to `src` and pass on a clean tree; gates consuming built `lib/` declare that dependency ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n117\t- **Keep compiler faces explicit.** Each package uses one aggregate except `api/remotes`; repo-wide programs seed a face config, never the root solution ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n118\t- **An empty `catch` names what it swallows** and why nothing else can reach it; keep the `try` to one statement.\n119\t- Do not comment on facts obvious from code.\n120\t- **Prefer symmetry for parallel values**; unexplained asymmetry usually signals a missed extraction.\n121\t- **Tests describe behavior, not correctness.** Change obsolete behavior with its tests; explain why in the PR.\n122\t- **Non-trivial changes MUST include an Agent Note in the same PR;** only mechanical/local edits are exempt ([scope](.agents/notes/README.md#when-to-write-one)). Archived notes are frozen: never edit or treat them as current authority ([archive policy](.agents/notes/README.md#archiving-and-deletion)).\n123\t- **Testing policy** — [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md). Every non-trivial model- or product-user-visible behavior change adds or updates a keyless snapshot through a real runnable example in the same PR; package tests, e2e-only assertions, and mock-only fixtures do not substitute for the assembled application transcript. Fixtures must replay on macOS/Linux; fix fixtures, not normalizers.\n124\t- **A tool's UI render intent is part of its design**, decided up front (`generic`/`terminal`/`diff`, `locations`); presentation methods are pure functions of `args` ([cookbook](docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md)).\n125\t- **Plan unit, e2e, and snapshot coverage** for capability seams, lifecycle paths, and transcript output; include missing snapshot-harness support in the same change.\n126\t- **Choose PR history deliberately.** Split independent changes; fix the introducing PR before propagation. Standalone PRs and official stacks may merge-forward or rebase after review. Rewrites use `--force-with-lease`, abort on remote movement, never raw `--force`; an in-progress merge-forward preserves its checkpoint before taking a newer base ([rationale](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-02-native-github-stacks-and-optional-rebases.md)).\n127\t- **Labels:** one PR `kind/*`, all material `area/*`, and native Issue Type ([taxonomy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-08-unified-github-label-taxonomy.md)).\n128\t- TODO markers: `FIXME`/`TODO`/`XXX` by urgency ([semantics](docs/development.md)).\n129\t- Files end with exactly one trailing newline; `git diff --cached --check` (pre-commit) gates it.\n130\t\n131\t## Defensive patterns\n132\t\n133\tRead [docs/defensive-patterns.md](docs/defensive-patterns.md) before lifecycle, concurrency, subprocess, or teardown work.\n134\t\n135\t## Type safety and documentation\n136\t\n137\tEverything compiles under `strict: true` with `noImplicitAny`; every remaining `any` explains why narrowing is infeasible. Every module and export has concise JSDoc for its non-obvious contract; function-like exports include `@param`/`@returns`, as enforced by `verify-export-jsdoc`. Heritage-declared members, plugin-protocol slots, and constructors keep their docs at the declaring Service Definition, protocol, or class.\n138\t\n139\tComments and docs state complete contracts and context, not reasoning transcripts. Use direct, concrete terms. Do not use metaphors. Before writing `contract`, `boundary`, or `shape`, ask whether a more exact term names the subject: write `response fields`, `JSON validation`, or `ESM exports` instead of `response shape`, `validation boundary`, or `module shape`. Keep `contract` for preconditions, postconditions, invariants, compatibility promises, and other obligations that callers, callees, implementers, providers, producers, or consumers rely on. Keep a literal process, wire, security, transaction, or lifecycle boundary. Do not narrate control flow or tests, preserve review history, or restate code. Keep behavior, failure, timing, ownership, and safe-use facts; link the rationale. Use [dsh-prose-standard](.agents/skills/dsh-prose-standard/SKILL.md) for decisions. Wire mechanically checkable invariants into an executed top-level gate and prove each changed acceptance path rejects an invalid case. Use narrow, justified exceptions instead of disabling a rule globally.\n140\t\n141\tDocs accompany every code change: update affected README and JSDoc contracts together. Routine bilingual work follows [docs/AGENTS.md](docs/AGENTS.md); only explicit user invocation may run `dsh-translate-docs`. Current-state prose, one physical line per paragraph, one home per fact, and word budgets live there.\n142\t\n143\t## Editing these instructions\n144\t\n145\t`CLAUDE.md` symlinks `AGENTS.md` at root, `packages/`, and `examples/`; edit the real file. Protocol constants, external specs, and security invariants stay fixed.\n- **Misconfiguration fails loud** at load when self-contained, otherwise at the earliest resolvable point; never silently skip a missing referent.\n- **Opaque cross-boundary ids are branded** (`Branded<B>` from `dsh-brand`), never bare `string`.\n- **Trust TypeScript at typed same-process boundaries.** Do not add runtime validation, fallback behavior, or hostile-input tests solely for values the static interface requires; validate at parser/config, queued, model/tool JSON, durable/file, worker, process, and wire boundaries.\n- **Source plane vs artifact plane, never mixed.** Static gates and tests resolve workspace imports through tsconfig `paths` to `src` and pass on a clean tree; gates consuming built `lib/` declare that dependency ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **Keep compiler faces explicit.** Each package uses one aggregate except `api/remotes`; repo-wide programs seed a face config, never the root solution ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **An empty `catch` names what it swallows** and why nothing else can reach it; keep the `try` to one statement.\n- Do not comment on facts obvious from code.\n- **Prefer symmetry for parallel values**; unexplained asymmetry usually signals a missed extraction.\n- **Tests describe behavior, not correctness.** Change obsolete behavior with its tests; explain why in the PR.\n- **Non-trivial changes MUST include an Agent Note in the same PR;** only mechanical/local edits are exempt ([scope](.agents/notes/README.md#when-to-write-one)). Archived notes are frozen: never edit or treat them as current authority ([archive policy](.agents/notes/README.md#archiving-and-deletion)).\n- **Testing policy** — [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md). Every non-trivial model- or product-user-visible behavior change adds or updates a keyless snapshot through a real runnable example in the same PR; package tests, e2e-only assertions, and mock-only fixtures do not substitute for the assembled application transcript. Fixtures must replay on macOS/Linux; fix fixtures, not normalizers.\n- **A tool's UI render intent is part of its design**, decided up front (`generic`/`terminal`/`diff`, `locations`); presentation methods are pure functions of `args` ([cookbook](docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md)).\n- **Plan unit, e2e, and snapshot coverage** for capability seams, lifecycle paths, and transcript output; include missing snapshot-harness support in the same change.\n- **Choose PR history deliberately.** Split independent changes; fix the introducing PR before propagation. Standalone PRs and official stacks may merge-forward or rebase after review. Rewrites use `--force-with-lease`, abort on remote movement, never raw `--force`; an in-progress merge-forward preserves its checkpoint before taking a newer base ([rationale](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-02-native-github-stacks-and-optional-rebases.md)).\n- **Labels:** one PR `kind/*`, all material `area/*`, and native Issue Type ([taxonomy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-08-unified-github-label-taxonomy.md)).\n- TODO markers: `FIXME`/`TODO`/`XXX` by urgency ([semantics](docs/development.md)).\n- Files end with exactly one trailing newline; `git diff --cached --check` (pre-commit) gates it.\n\n## Defensive patterns\n\nRead [docs/defensive-patterns.md](docs/defensive-patterns.md) before lifecycle, concurrency, subprocess, or teardown work.\n\n## Type safety and documentation\n\nEverything compiles under `strict: true` with `noImplicitAny`; every remaining `any` explains why narrowing is infeasible. Every module and export has concise JSDoc for its non-obvious contract; function-like exports include `@param`/`@returns`, as enforced by `verify-export-jsdoc`. Heritage-declared members, plugin-protocol slots, and constructors keep their docs at the declaring Service Definition, protocol, or class.\n\nComments and docs state complete contracts and context, not reasoning transcripts. Use direct, concrete terms. Do not use metaphors. Before writing `contract`, `boundary`, or `shape`, ask whether a more exact term names the subject: write `response fields`, `JSON validation`, or `ESM exports` instead of `response shape`, `validation boundary`, or `module shape`. Keep `contract` for preconditions, postconditions, invariants, compatibility promises, and other obligations that callers, callees, implementers, providers, producers, or consumers rely on. Keep a literal process, wire, security, transaction, or lifecycle boundary. Do not narrate control flow or tests, preserve review history, or restate code. Keep behavior, failure, timing, ownership, and safe-use facts; link the rationale. Use [dsh-prose-standard](.agents/skills/dsh-prose-standard/SKILL.md) for decisions. Wire mechanically checkable invariants into an executed top-level gate and prove each changed acceptance path rejects an invalid case. Use narrow, justified exceptions instead of disabling a rule globally.\n\nDocs accompany every code change: update affected README and JSDoc contracts together. Routine bilingual work follows [docs/AGENTS.md](docs/AGENTS.md); only explicit user invocation may run `dsh-translate-docs`. Current-state prose, one physical line per paragraph, one home per fact, and word budgets live there.\n\n## Editing these instructions\n\n`CLAUDE.md` symlinks `AGENTS.md` at root, `packages/`, and `examples/`; edit the real file. Keep each rule self-contained while linking high-level docs. Condense when clarity survives; raise a `verify-doc-budgets` ceiling when the required content genuinely needs more space.\n\n## Vendoring policy\n\n`vendor/` packages are pinned source copies (manifest with upstream SHAs in [vendor/README.md](vendor/README.md)). Update via the sync procedure there; re-apply or retire the logged local modifications; rerun `pnpm run test && pnpm run build`.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"b7d30922-7582-40a6-b02c-1f5b24684a68","timestamp":"2026-09-08T01:23:15.314Z","rendered":[{"content":"<system-reminder>\nContents of /home/user/deepseek-harness/.claude/worktrees/agent-ad8981f5014a7045d/CLAUDE.md:\n\n# AGENTS.md\n\nDeepSeek Harness is a plugin-based agent harness on vendored Cordis: **everything is a plugin**. build outputs and safe residue from deleted packages\npnpm run test           # vitest unit tests\npnpm run test:coverage  # CI coverage gate: per-file 100% on packages/*/*/src\npnpm run test:e2e       # real-API tests; self-skip without DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\npnpm run test:snapshot  # keyless ACP/headless replay vs expected outputs; filter: -t <name>\npnpm run test:snapshot:record  # re-record expected outputs (needs key)\npnpm run typecheck\npnpm run lint\npnpm run duplication    # cross-file TypeScript clone detection\npnpm run build          # tsc emits lib/types, tsdown bundles runtime\npnpm run hygiene        # knip + publint + workspace constraints + NodeNext consumer check\npnpm run check:windows-wine  # ONLY when diagnosing a known Windows failure (needs wine); CI owns this signal\npnpm run doc-sync       # all documentation gates; leaf list in scripts/run-gates.ts\npnpm run website:build  # VitePress build (doubles as dead-link check)\npnpm dsh --profile headless \"task\"  # run one task from source (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\npnpm run demo:cordis    # the agent modifies its own runtime (needs key)\npnpm run demo:acp       # ACP automation server (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\n```\n\n### Host sandbox failures\n\nWhen required `gh`, `pnpm`, build, test, or generator commands fail because the agent sandbox blocks credentials, network, IPC, file watching, or nested `sandbox-exec`, retry unchanged with the narrowest host escalation before diagnosing authentication or project failure. Require sandbox evidence; never bypass genuine test failures or the product sandbox under test.\n\n### Run relevant checks locally\n\nRun checks before pushes via [dsh-pre-push-checks](.agents/skills/dsh-pre-push-checks/SKILL.md); report only commands run. After `gh stack sync`, validate immediately; do not merge before checks pass.\n\n- Match evidence to the surface: focused tests for behavior, snapshots for model or user output, `doc-sync` for docs, build/hygiene and built smokes for published paths, and real-API e2e for provider behavior.\n- Never default to the full suite or repeat a passing check for commit or push. CI owns exhaustive coverage and the platform matrix; rehearse all locally only by explicit request, for CI diagnosis, or for an irreducibly repository-wide change.\n- `test:coverage`, not `test`, is the CI coverage gate ([why](docs/testing.md)).\n\n## Secrets / .env\n\nReal-API tests and demos read `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, optional `DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL`, and root `.env`. cordis.yml allows `!!js` (never `!js`) under plugin `config` and entry `disabled`; other metadata stays literal, so conditional composition also uses overlays ([primer](docs/cordis-primer.md#loader-configuration)). Never commit credentials. CI e2e skips without a key; [testing.md](docs/testing.md) owns key policy.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- Every npm package is `@deepseek-ai/dsh-<name>`; vendored packages are rescoped ([mapping](docs/rescope.md)) and `private: true`. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` is a peerDependency (+ dev) of every harness package.\n- ESM everywhere (`\"type\": \"module\"`). Use package names across packages and `.ts` in local relative imports. The `dsh` CLI source launch runs through tsx's ESM-only hook (`node --import tsx/esm`); modules it reaches must stay ESM (no CJS-only exports) — Node's native TypeScript modes are unavailable across the engines range ([source-launch contract](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-07-29-dsh-source-launch-tsx-esm.md)). Never commit credentials. CI e2e skips without a key; [testing.md](docs/testing.md) owns key policy.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- Every npm package is `@deepseek-ai/dsh-<name>`; vendored packages are rescoped ([mapping](docs/rescope.md)) and `private: true`. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` is a peerDependency (+ dev) of every harness package.\n- ESM everywhere (`\"type\": \"module\"`). Use package names across packages and `.ts` in local relative imports. Config subprocesses run built `lib/` under plain Node; source regressions use their declared launcher ([testing policy](docs/testing.md#test-subprocess-launch-modes)). The `dsh` CLI source launch runs through tsx's ESM-only hook (`node --import tsx/esm`); modules it reaches must stay ESM (no CJS-only exports) — Node's native TypeScript modes are unavailable across the engines range ([source-launch contract](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-07-29-dsh-source-launch-tsx-esm.md)). Raw/Web `cordis.yml` bare plugins must appear in their resolver manifest's `dependencies`; `verify-cordis-config` enforces it.\n- **Registrations are effects**: every contribution goes through `ctx.effect()` / `ctx.on()`; a registry's `register()` returns the disposer.\n- **Runtime invariants assert owned relationships.** Check authoritative event streams or mutable data, not service or method presence, plugin metadata or effects, or fixed pure examples. Without a plausible relationship, an explained empty companion is correct ([package invariant rules](packages/AGENTS.md)).\n- **Typed events use declaration merging** and merge-extensible maps. Event JSDoc needs `@mode` and payload `@param`; scoped keys absent from payloads need `@dshScopeScan unsupported`. Public service methods document parameters and non-void returns. A `SessionEventMap` member is required-on-read by default — builds that do not know its type refuse the log unless the event carries the envelope's `ignorable: true`; only structural format changes bump `SESSION_FORMAT_VERSION` ([mechanism](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-08-10-session-log-version-mechanism.md)).\n- **Switch on discriminant tags.** Closed unions end in `assertNever`; merge-extensible unions fall through a documented default.\n- **Waterfall listeners MUST call `next()`** to delegate; returning without it short-circuits the chain ([semantics](docs/cordis-primer.md#cordis-waterfall-semantics)).\n- **Model-visible ⟺ logged**: anything that reaches a model request must be reconstructable from the session log; a new model-visible input requires a session event.\n- **Plugins, not loop changes**: new behavior goes on documented extension points; changing `agent-loop` requires updating docs/architecture.md.\n- **A capability seam comprises Service Definition / Service Provider / Consumer roles.** It is complete, never one role; split only when roles evolve independently ([glossary](docs/glossary.md#capability-seam)).\n- **Prefer maintained dependencies over hand-rolling** when they genuinely delete owned code and tests ([policy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-07-26-dependencies-over-hand-rolling.md)).\n- **Explicit > implicit at package boundaries**: defaulting is an explicit `resolve(request): Spec` step in the owning implementation, never a hidden `?? default` inside `run()` (the `dsh-shell` request/spec split is the template).\n- **No hardcoded tunables in plugins**: deployment-varying choices are validated `Config` fields changeable from cordis.yml; a `DEFAULT_*` constant or test hook is not configurability. Protocol constants, external specs, and security invariants stay fixed.\n- **Misconfiguration fails loud** at load when self-contained, otherwise at the earliest resolvable point; never silently skip a missing referent.\n- **Opaque cross-boundary ids are branded** (`Branded<B>` from `dsh-brand`), never bare `string`.\n- **Trust TypeScript at typed same-process boundaries.** Do not add runtime validation, fallback behavior, or hostile-input tests solely for values the static interface requires; validate at parser/config, queued, model/tool JSON, durable/file, worker, process, and wire boundaries.\n- **Source plane vs artifact plane, never mixed.** Static gates and tests resolve workspace imports through tsconfig `paths` to `src` and pass on a clean tree; gates consuming built `lib/` declare that dependency ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **Keep compiler faces explicit.** Each package uses one aggregate except `api/remotes`; repo-wide programs seed a face config, never the root solution ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **An empty `catch` names what it swallows** and why nothing else can reach it; keep the `try` to one statement.\n- Do not comment on facts obvious from code.\n- **Prefer symmetry for parallel values**; unexplained asymmetry usually signals a missed extraction.\n- **Tests describe behavior, not correctness.** Change obsolete behavior with its tests; explain why in the PR.\n- **Non-trivial changes MUST include an Agent Note in the same PR;** only mechanical/local edits are exempt ([scope](.agents/notes/README.md#when-to-write-one)). Archived notes are frozen: never edit or treat them as current authority ([archive policy](.agents/notes/README.md#archiving-and-deletion)).\n- **Testing policy** — [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md). Every non-trivial model- or product-user-visible behavior change adds or updates a keyless snapshot through a real runnable example in the same PR; package tests, e2e-only assertions, and mock-only fixtures do not substitute for the assembled application transcript. Fixtures must replay on macOS/Linux; fix fixtures, not normalizers.\n- **A tool's UI render intent is part of its design**, decided up front (`generic`/`terminal`/`diff`, `locations`); presentation methods are pure functions of `args` ([cookbook](docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md)).\n- **Plan unit, e2e, and snapshot coverage** for capability seams, lifecycle paths, and transcript output; include missing snapshot-harness support in the same change.\n- **Choose PR history deliberately.** Split independent changes; fix the introducing PR before propagation. Standalone PRs and official stacks may merge-forward or rebase after review. Rewrites use `--force-with-lease`, abort on remote movement, never raw `--force`; an in-progress merge-forward preserves its checkpoint before taking a newer base ([rationale](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-02-native-github-stacks-and-optional-rebases.md)).\n- **Labels:** one PR `kind/*`, all material `area/*`, and native Issue Type ([taxonomy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-08-unified-github-label-taxonomy.md)).\n- TODO markers: `FIXME`/`TODO`/`XXX` by urgency ([semantics](docs/development.md)).\n- Files end with exactly one trailing newline; `git diff --cached --check` (pre-commit) gates it.\n\n## Defensive patterns\n\nRead [docs/defensive-patterns.md](docs/defensive-patterns.md) before lifecycle, concurrency, subprocess, or teardown work.\n\n## Type safety and documentation\n\nEverything compiles under `strict: true` with `noImplicitAny`; every remaining `any` explains why narrowing is infeasible. Every module and export has concise JSDoc for its non-obvious contract; function-like exports include `@param`/`@returns`, as enforced by `verify-export-jsdoc`. Heritage-declared members, plugin-protocol slots, and constructors keep their docs at the declaring Service Definition, protocol, or class.\n\nComments and docs state complete contracts and context, not reasoning transcripts. Use direct, concrete terms. Do not use metaphors. Before writing `contract`, `boundary`, or `shape`, ask whether a more exact term names the subject: write `response fields`, `JSON validation`, or `ESM exports` instead of `response shape`, `validation boundary`, or `module shape`. Keep `contract` for preconditions, postconditions, invariants, compatibility promises, and other obligations that callers, callees, implementers, providers, producers, or consumers rely on. Keep behavior, failure, timing, ownership, and safe-use facts; link the rationale. Use [dsh-prose-standard](.agents/skills/dsh-prose-standard/SKILL.md) for decisions. Read [docs/architecture.md](docs/architecture.md) before changing `packages/`; follow [docs/AGENTS.md](docs/AGENTS.md) for documentation.\n\n## Pre-release stance: foundation over blast radius\n\n**Remove this section at the first tagged release.** With no external consumers, prefer the correct foundation over compatibility shims: rename or repackage freely and update every reference together. CLI/ACP/JSON-RPC bins)\n  support/     dev/test infrastructure\n  util/        zero-dependency utilities\npython/      Python SDK and bundled runtime (see python/README.md)\nnative/      @deepseek-ai/node-addon-landlock-run source of record (see native/README.md)\nexamples/    Runnable cordis.yml leaves over packages/examples bundles (see examples/AGENTS.md)\n.agents/     Agent workflows and Agent Notes (`notes/`)\ndocs/        architecture, generated catalogs, postmortems, cookbook (see docs/AGENTS.md)\nscripts/     repo gates and generators\nwebsite/     VitePress projection of selected bilingual docs/ sources\n```\n\nPackage groups: [packages/README.md](packages/README.md).\n\n## Commands\n\n```sh\npnpm install            # pnpm workspaces, node ^22.19 || >=24\npnpm run clean           # remove build outputs and safe residue from deleted packages\npnpm run test           # vitest unit tests\npnpm run test:coverage  # CI coverage gate: per-file 100% on packages/*/*/src\npnpm run test:e2e       # real-API tests; self-skip without DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\npnpm run test:snapshot  # keyless ACP/headless replay vs expected outputs; filter: -t <name>\npnpm run test:snapshot:record  # re-record expected outputs (needs key)\npnpm run typecheck\npnpm run lint\npnpm run duplication    # cross-file TypeScript clone detection\npnpm run build          # tsc emits lib/types, tsdown bundles runtime\npnpm run hygiene        # knip + publint + workspace constraints + NodeNext consumer check\npnpm run check:windows-wine  # ONLY when diagnosing a known Windows failure (needs wine); CI owns this signal\npnpm run doc-sync       # all documentation gates; leaf list in scripts/run-gates.ts\npnpm run website:build  # VitePress build (doubles as dead-link check)\npnpm dsh --profile headless \"task\"  # run one task from source (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\npnpm run demo:cordis    # the agent modifies its own runtime (needs key)\npnpm run demo:acp       # ACP automation server (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\n```\n\n### Host sandbox failures\n\nWhen required `gh`, `pnpm`, build, test, or generator commands fail because the agent sandbox blocks credentials, network, IPC, file watching, or nested `sandbox-exec`, retry unchanged with the narrowest host escalation before diagnosing authentication or project failure. Require sandbox evidence; never bypass genuine test failures or the product sandbox under test.\n\n### Run relevant checks locally\n\nRun checks before pushes via [dsh-pre-push-checks](.agents/skills/dsh-pre-push-checks/SKILL.md); report only commands run. After `gh stack sync`, validate immediately; do not merge before checks pass.\n\n- Match evidence to the surface: focused tests for behavior, snapshots for model or user output, `doc-sync` for docs, build/hygiene and built smokes for published paths, and real-API e2e for provider behavior.\n- Never default to the full suite or repeat a passing check for commit or push. CI owns exhaustive coverage and the platform matrix; rehearse all locally only by explicit request, for CI diagnosis, or for an irreducibly repository-wide change.\n- `test:coverage`, not `test`, is the CI coverage gate ([why](docs/testing.md)).\n\n## Secrets / .env\n\nReal-API tests and demos read `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, optional `DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL`, and root `.env`. cordis.yml allows `!!js` (never `!js`) under plugin `config` and entry `disabled`; other metadata stays literal, so conditional composition also uses overlays ([primer](docs/cordis-primer.md#loader-configuration)). Never commit credentials. CI e2e skips without a key; [testing.md](docs/testing.md) owns key policy.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- Every npm package is `@deepseek-ai/dsh-<name>`; vendored packages are rescoped ([mapping](docs/rescope.md)) and `private: true`. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` is a peerDependency (+ dev) of every harness package.\n- ESM everywhere (`\"type\": \"module\"`). Use package names across packages and `.ts` in local relative imports. Config subprocesses run built `lib/` under plain Node; source regressions use their declared launcher ([testing policy](docs/testing.md#test-subprocess-launch-modes)). The `dsh` CLI source launch runs through tsx's ESM-only hook (`node --import tsx/esm`); modules it reaches must stay ESM (no CJS-only exports) — Node's native TypeScript modes are unavailable across the engines range ([source-launch contract](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-07-29-dsh-source-launch-tsx-esm.md)). Raw/Web `cordis.yml` bare plugins must appear in their resolver manifest's `dependencies`; `verify-cordis-config` enforces it.\n- **Registrations are effects**: every contribution goes through `ctx.effect()` / `ctx.on()`; a registry's `register()` returns the disposer.\n- **Runtime invariants assert owned relationships.** Check authoritative event streams or mutable data, not service or method presence, plugin metadata or effects, or fixed pure examples. Without a plausible relationship, an explained empty companion is correct ([package invariant rules](packages/AGENTS.md)).\n- **Typed events use declaration merging** and merge-extensible maps. Event JSDoc needs `@mode` and payload `@param`; scoped keys absent from payloads need `@dshScopeScan unsupported`. Public service methods document parameters and non-void returns. A `SessionEventMap` member is required-on-read by default — builds that do not know its type refuse the log unless the event carries the envelope's `ignorable: true`; only structural format changes bump `SESSION_FORMAT_VERSION` ([mechanism](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-08-10-session-log-version-mechanism.md)).\n- **Switch on discriminant tags.** Closed unions end in `assertNever`; merge-extensible unions fall through a documented default.\n- **Waterfall listeners MUST call `next()`** to delegate; returning without it short-circuits the chain ([semantics](docs/cordis-primer.md#cordis-waterfall-semantics)).\n- **Model-visible ⟺ logged**: anything that reaches a model request must be reconstructable from the session log; a new model-visible input requires a session event.\n- **Plugins, not loop changes**: new behavior goes on documented extension points; changing `agent-loop` requires updating docs/architecture.md.\n- **A capability seam comprises Service Definition / Service Provider / Consumer roles.** It is complete, never one role; split only when roles evolve independently ([glossary](docs/glossary.md#capability-seam)).\n- **Prefer maintained dependencies over hand-rolling** when they genuinely delete owned code and tests ([policy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-07-26-dependencies-over-hand-rolling.md)).\n- **Explicit > implicit at package boundaries**: defaulting is an explicit `resolve(request): Spec` step in the owning implementation, never a hidden `?? default` inside `run()` (the `dsh-shell` request/spec split is the template).\n- **No hardcoded tunables in plugins**: deployment-varying choices are validated `Config` fields changeable from cordis.yml; a `DEFAULT_*` constant or test hook is not configurability. Protocol constants, external specs, and security invariants stay fixed.\n- **Misconfiguration fails loud** at load when self-contained, otherwise at the earliest resolvable point; never silently skip a missing referent.\n- **Opaque cross-boundary ids are branded** (`Branded<B>` from `dsh-brand`), never bare `string`.\n- **Trust TypeScript at typed same-process boundaries.** Do not add runtime validation, fallback behavior, or hostile-input tests solely for values the static interface requires; validate at parser/config, queued, model/tool JSON, durable/file, worker, process, and wire boundaries.\n- **Source plane vs artifact plane, never mixed.** Static gates and tests resolve workspace imports through tsconfig `paths` to `src` and pass on a clean tree; gates consuming built `lib/` declare that dependency ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **Keep compiler faces explicit.** Each package uses one aggregate except `api/remotes`; repo-wide programs seed a face config, never the root solution ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **An empty `catch` names what it swallows** and why nothing else can reach it; keep the `try` to one statement.\n- Do not comment on facts obvious from code.\n- **Prefer symmetry for parallel values**; unexplained asymmetry usually signals a missed extraction.\n- **Tests describe behavior, not correctness.** Change obsolete behavior with its tests; explain why in the PR.\n- **Non-trivial changes MUST include an Agent Note in the same PR;** only mechanical/local edits are exempt ([scope](.agents/notes/README.md#when-to-write-one)). Archived notes are frozen: never edit or treat them as current authority ([archive policy](.agents/notes/README.md#archiving-and-deletion)).\n- **Testing policy** — [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md). Every non-trivial model- or product-user-visible behavior change adds or updates a keyless snapshot through a real runnable example in the same PR; package tests, e2e-only assertions, and mock-only fixtures do not substitute for the assembled application transcript. Fixtures must replay on macOS/Linux; fix fixtures, not normalizers.\n- **A tool's UI render intent is part of its design**, decided up front (`generic`/`terminal`/`diff`, `locations`); presentation methods are pure functions of `args` ([cookbook](docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md)).\n- **Plan unit, e2e, and snapshot coverage** for capability seams, lifecycle paths, and transcript output; include missing snapshot-harness support in the same change.\n- **Choose PR history deliberately.** Split independent changes; fix the introducing PR before propagation. Standalone PRs and official stacks may merge-forward or rebase after review. Rewrites use `--force-with-lease`, abort on remote movement, never raw `--force`; an in-progress merge-forward preserves its checkpoint before taking a newer base ([rationale](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-02-native-github-stacks-and-optional-rebases.md)).\n- **Labels:** one PR `kind/*`, all material `area/*`, and native Issue Type ([taxonomy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-08-unified-github-label-taxonomy.md)).\n- TODO markers: `FIXME`/`TODO`/`XXX` by urgency ([semantics](docs/development.md)).\n- Files end with exactly one trailing newline; `git diff --cached --check` (pre-commit) gates it.\n\n## Defensive patterns\n\nRead [docs/defensive-patterns.md](docs/defensive-patterns.md) before lifecycle, concurrency, subprocess, or teardown work.\n\n## Type safety and documentation\n\nEverything compiles under `strict: true` with `noImplicitAny`; every remaining `any` explains why narrowing is infeasible. Every module and export has concise JSDoc for its non-obvious contract; function-like exports include `@param`/`@returns`, as enforced by `verify-export-jsdoc`. Heritage-declared members, plugin-protocol slots, and constructors keep their docs at the declaring Service Definition, protocol, or class.\n\nComments and docs state complete contracts and context, not reasoning transcripts. Use direct, concrete terms. Do not use metaphors. Before writing `contract`, `boundary`, or `shape`, ask whether a more exact term names the subject: write `response fields`, `JSON validation`, or `ESM exports` instead of `response shape`, `validation boundary`, or `module shape`. Keep `contract` for preconditions, postconditions, invariants, compatibility promises, and other obligations that callers, callees, implementers, providers, producers, or consumers rely on. Keep a literal process, wire, security, transaction, or lifecycle boundary. Do not narrate control flow or tests, preserve review history, or restate code. Keep behavior, failure, timing, ownership, and safe-use facts; link the rationale. Use [dsh-prose-standard](.agents/skills/dsh-prose-standard/SKILL.md) for decisions. Wire mechanically checkable invariants into an executed top-level gate and prove each changed acceptance path rejects an invalid case. Use narrow, justified exceptions instead of disabling a rule globally.\n\nDocs accompany every code change: update affected README and JSDoc contracts together. Routine bilingual work follows [docs/AGENTS.md](docs/AGENTS.md); only explicit user invocation may run `dsh-translate-docs`. Current-state prose, one physical line per paragraph, one home per fact, and word budgets live there.\n\n## Editing these instructions\n\n`CLAUDE.md` symlinks `AGENTS.md` at root, `packages/`, and `examples/`; edit the real file. Keep each rule self-contained while linking high-level docs. Rewrites use `--force-with-lease`, abort on remote movement, never raw `--force`; an in-progress merge-forward preserves its checkpoint before taking a newer base ([rationale](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-02-native-github-stacks-and-optional-rebases.md)).\n- **Labels:** one PR `kind/*`, all material `area/*`, and native Issue Type ([taxonomy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-08-08-unified-github-label-taxonomy.md)).\n- TODO markers: `FIXME`/`TODO`/`XXX` by urgency ([semantics](docs/development.md)).\n- Files end with exactly one trailing newline; `git diff --cached --check` (pre-commit) gates it.\n\n## Defensive patterns\n\nRead [docs/defensive-patterns.md](docs/defensive-patterns.md) before lifecycle, concurrency, subprocess, or teardown work.\n\n## Type safety and documentation\n\nEverything compiles under `strict: true` with `noImplicitAny`; every remaining `any` explains why narrowing is infeasible. Every module and export has concise JSDoc for its non-obvious contract; function-like exports include `@param`/`@returns`, as enforced by `verify-export-jsdoc`. >=24\npnpm run clean           # remove build outputs and safe residue from deleted packages\npnpm run test           # vitest unit tests\npnpm run test:coverage  # CI coverage gate: per-file 100% on packages/*/*/src\npnpm run test:e2e       # real-API tests; self-skip without DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\npnpm run test:snapshot  # keyless ACP/headless replay vs expected outputs; filter: -t <name>\npnpm run test:snapshot:record  # re-record expected outputs (needs key)\npnpm run typecheck\npnpm run lint\npnpm run duplication    # cross-file TypeScript clone detection\npnpm run build          # tsc emits lib/types, tsdown bundles runtime\npnpm run hygiene        # knip + publint + workspace constraints + NodeNext consumer check\npnpm run check:windows-wine  # ONLY when diagnosing a known Windows failure (needs wine); CI owns this signal\npnpm run doc-sync       # all documentation gates; leaf list in scripts/run-gates.ts\npnpm run website:build  # VitePress build (doubles as dead-link check)\npnpm dsh --profile headless \"task\"  # run one task from source (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\npnpm run demo:cordis    # the agent modifies its own runtime (needs key)\npnpm run demo:acp       # ACP automation server (needs DEEPSEEK_API_KEY)\n```\n\n### Host sandbox failures\n\nWhen required `gh`, `pnpm`, build, test, or generator commands fail because the agent sandbox blocks credentials, network, IPC, file watching, or nested `sandbox-exec`, retry unchanged with the narrowest host escalation before diagnosing authentication or project failure. Require sandbox evidence; never bypass genuine test failures or the product sandbox under test.\n\n### Run relevant checks locally\n\nRun checks before pushes via [dsh-pre-push-checks](.agents/skills/dsh-pre-push-checks/SKILL.md); report only commands run. After `gh stack sync`, validate immediately; do not merge before checks pass.\n\n- Match evidence to the surface: focused tests for behavior, snapshots for model or user output, `doc-sync` for docs, build/hygiene and built smokes for published paths, and real-API e2e for provider behavior.\n- Never default to the full suite or repeat a passing check for commit or push. CI owns exhaustive coverage and the platform matrix; rehearse all locally only by explicit request, for CI diagnosis, or for an irreducibly repository-wide change.\n- `test:coverage`, not `test`, is the CI coverage gate ([why](docs/testing.md)).\n\n## Secrets / .env\n\nReal-API tests and demos read `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, optional `DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL`, and root `.env`. cordis.yml allows `!!js` (never `!js`) under plugin `config` and entry `disabled`; other metadata stays literal, so conditional composition also uses overlays ([primer](docs/cordis-primer.md#loader-configuration)). Never commit credentials. CI e2e skips without a key; [testing.md](docs/testing.md) owns key policy.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- Every npm package is `@deepseek-ai/dsh-<name>`; vendored packages are rescoped ([mapping](docs/rescope.md)) and `private: true`. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` is a peerDependency (+ dev) of every harness package.\n- ESM everywhere (`\"type\": \"module\"`). Use package names across packages and `.ts` in local relative imports. Config subprocesses run built `lib/` under plain Node; source regressions use their declared launcher ([testing policy](docs/testing.md#test-subprocess-launch-modes)). The `dsh` CLI source launch runs through tsx's ESM-only hook (`node --import tsx/esm`); modules it reaches must stay ESM (no CJS-only exports) — Node's native TypeScript modes are unavailable across the engines range ([source-launch contract](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-07-29-dsh-source-launch-tsx-esm.md)). Raw/Web `cordis.yml` bare plugins must appear in their resolver manifest's `dependencies`; `verify-cordis-config` enforces it.\n- **Registrations are effects**: every contribution goes through `ctx.effect()` / `ctx.on()`; a registry's `register()` returns the disposer.\n- **Runtime invariants assert owned relationships.** Check authoritative event streams or mutable data, not service or method presence, plugin metadata or effects, or fixed pure examples. Without a plausible relationship, an explained empty companion is correct ([package invariant rules](packages/AGENTS.md)).\n- **Typed events use declaration merging** and merge-extensible maps. Event JSDoc needs `@mode` and payload `@param`; scoped keys absent from payloads need `@dshScopeScan unsupported`. Public service methods document parameters and non-void returns. A `SessionEventMap` member is required-on-read by default — builds that do not know its type refuse the log unless the event carries the envelope's `ignorable: true`; only structural format changes bump `SESSION_FORMAT_VERSION` ([mechanism](.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-08-10-session-log-version-mechanism.md)).\n- **Switch on discriminant tags.** Closed unions end in `assertNever`; merge-extensible unions fall through a documented default.\n- **Waterfall listeners MUST call `next()`** to delegate; returning without it short-circuits the chain ([semantics](docs/cordis-primer.md#cordis-waterfall-semantics)).\n- **Model-visible ⟺ logged**: anything that reaches a model request must be reconstructable from the session log; a new model-visible input requires a session event.\n- **Plugins, not loop changes**: new behavior goes on documented extension points; changing `agent-loop` requires updating docs/architecture.md.\n- **A capability seam comprises Service Definition / Service Provider / Consumer roles.** It is complete, never one role; split only when roles evolve independently ([glossary](docs/glossary.md#capability-seam)).\n- **Prefer maintained dependencies over hand-rolling** when they genuinely delete owned code and tests ([policy](.agents/notes/implemented/process/2026-07-26-dependencies-over-hand-rolling.md)).\n- **Explicit > implicit at package boundaries**: defaulting is an explicit `resolve(request): Spec` step in the owning implementation, never a hidden `?? default` inside `run()` (the `dsh-shell` request/spec split is the template).\n- **No hardcoded tunables in plugins**: deployment-varying choices are validated `Config` fields changeable from cordis.yml; a `DEFAULT_*` constant or test hook is not configurability. Protocol constants, external specs, and security invariants stay fixed.\n- **Misconfiguration fails loud** at load when self-contained, otherwise at the earliest resolvable point; never silently skip a missing referent.\n- **Opaque cross-boundary ids are branded** (`Branded<B>` from `dsh-brand`), never bare `string`.\n- **Trust TypeScript at typed same-process boundaries.** Do not add runtime validation, fallback behavior, or hostile-input tests solely for values the static interface requires; validate at parser/config, queued, model/tool JSON, durable/file, worker, process, and wire boundaries.\n- **Source plane vs artifact plane, never mixed.** Static gates and tests resolve workspace imports through tsconfig `paths` to `src` and pass on a clean tree; gates consuming built `lib/` declare that dependency ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **Keep compiler faces explicit.** Each package uses one aggregate except `api/remotes`; repo-wide programs seed a face config, never the root solution ([layout](docs/development.md#typescript-project-layout)).\n- **An empty `catch` names what it swallows** and why nothing else can reach it; keep the `try` to one statement.\n- Do not comment on facts obvious from code.\n- **Prefer symmetry for parallel values**; unexplained asymmetry usually signals a missed extraction.\n- **Tests describe behavior, not correctness.** Change obsolete behavior with its tests; explain why in the PR.\n- **Non-trivial changes MUST include an Agent Note in the same PR;** only mechanical/local edits are exempt ([scope](.agents/notes/README.md#when-to-write-one)). Archived notes are frozen: never edit or treat them as current authority ([archive policy](.agents/notes/README.md#archiving-and-deletion)).\n- **Testing policy** — [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md). Every non-trivial model- or product-user-visible behavior change adds or updates a keyless snapshot through a real runnable example in the same PR; package tests, e2e-only assertions, and mock-only fixtures do not substitute for the assembled application transcript. Fixtures must replay on macOS/Linux; fix fixtures, not normalizers.\n- **A tool's UI render intent is part of its design**, decided up front (`generic`/`terminal`/`diff`, `locations`); presentation methods are pure functions of `args` ([cookbook](docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md)).\n- **Plan unit, e2e, and snapshot coverage** for capability seams, lifecycle paths, and transcript output; include missing snapshot-harness support in the same change.\n- **Choose PR history deliberately.** Split independent changes; fix the introducing PR before propagation.
